= YAH - YOUTHS
:toc:

== YAH

* `Psa 68:4` Sing to God, sing praises to his name. Extol him who rides upon the heavens by his name, *_YAH_*, and rejoice before him.
* `Psa 89:8` O LORD God of hosts, who is mighty like you, O *_Yah_*? And your faithfulness is around you.
* `Isa 12:2` Behold, God is my salvation. I will trust and not be afraid, for *_Yah_*, YAHWEH, is my strength and my song. He also has become my salvation."

== YAHWEH

* `Gen 22:14` And Abraham called the name of that place *_Yahweh_* Will Provide, as it is said to this day, "On the mountain of the LORD it will be seen."
* `Exo 6:3` And I appeared to Abraham, to Isaac, and to Jacob by the name of God Almighty, but by my name, *_YAHWEH_*, I was not known to them.
* `Exo 15:3` The LORD is a man of war. *_Yahweh_* is his name.
* `Exo 17:15` And Moses built an altar and called its name *_Yahweh_* is My Banner,
* `Deu 6:4` "Hear, O Israel. The LORD our God, *_Yahweh_*, is one.
* `Deu 28:58` "If you will not observe to do all the words of this law that are written in this book so that you may fear this glorious and fearful name, *_YAHWEH_* YOUR GOD,
* `Jdg 6:24` Then Gideon built an altar there to the LORD and called it *_Yahweh_* is Peace. To this day it is still in Ophrah of the Abi-ezrites.
* `1Sa 6:20` And the men of Beth-shemesh said, "Who is able to stand before *_Yahweh_*, this holy God? And to whom shall he go up from us?"
* `Neh 9:6` You are *_Yahweh_*, even you alone. You have made heaven, the heaven of heavens with all their host, the earth and all things that are in it, the seas and all that is in them, and you preserve them all. And the host of heaven worships you.
* `Psa 8:1` [To the Chief Musician upon Gittith. A Psalm of David.] O *_Yahweh_* our Lord, how excellent your name is in all the earth! You have set your glory above the heavens.
* `Psa 8:9` O *_Yahweh_* our Lord, how excellent your name is in all the earth!
* `Psa 11:7` For *_Yahweh_* is righteous. He loves righteousness. His countenance beholds the upright.
* `Psa 83:18` So that men may know that you, whose name alone is *_YAHWEH_*, are the most high over all the earth.
* `Psa 85:8` I will hear what God, *_Yahweh_*, will speak, for he will speak peace to his people and to his saints. But do not let them turn back to folly.
* `Psa 109:21` But deal with me, O *_Yahweh_* the Lord, for your name's sake. Because your mercy is good, deliver me.
* `Isa 12:2` Behold, God is my salvation. I will trust and not be afraid, for Yah, *_YAHWEH_*, is my strength and my song. He also has become my salvation."
* `Isa 26:4` Trust in the LORD forever, for in the LORD *_YAHWEH_* is everlasting strength.
* `Isa 51:22` Thus says your Lord *_Yahweh_*, and your God who pleads the cause of his people: "Behold, I have taken out of your hand the cup of trembling, even the dregs of the cup of my fury. You shall no longer drink it again.
* `Jer 16:21` "Therefore, behold, this time I will cause them to know, I will cause them to know my hand and my might. And they shall know that my name is *_YAHWEH_*."
* `Jer 23:6` In his days Judah shall be saved, and Israel shall dwell in safety. And this is his name by which he shall be called: *_YAHWEH_* OUR RIGHTEOUSNESS.
* `Jer 32:18` You show loving-kindness to thousands and recompense the iniquity of the fathers into the bosom of their children after them. The Great, the Mighty God, *_YAHWEH_* of Hosts, is his name,
* `Jer 33:2` "Thus says the LORD, the maker of this, the LORD who formed it to establish it, *_Yahweh_* is his name:
* `Jer 33:16` In those days Judah shall be saved and Jerusalem shall dwell in safety. And this is the name by which she shall be called: *_YAHWEH_* Our Righteousness.'
* `Amo 4:13` For behold, he who forms the mountains, creates the wind, declares to man what is his thought, makes the morning darkness, and treads upon the high places of the earth, *_Yahweh_*, the God of hosts, is his name."
* `Amo 5:8` Seek him who makes the seven stars and Orion, turns the shadow of death into the morning, makes the day dark with night, who calls for the waters of the sea, and pours them out upon the face of the earth. *_YAHWEH_* is his name.
* `Amo 5:16` Therefore, *_Yahweh_*, the God of hosts, the Lord, says thus: "Wailing shall be in all streets, and they shall say in all the highways, 'Alas! Alas!' And they shall call the farmer to mourning and those who are skillful in lamentation to wailing.
* `Amo 9:6` It is he who builds his upper chambers in the heaven and has founded his vault on the earth, he who calls for the waters of the sea and pours them out upon the face of the earth—*_Yahweh_* is his name.
* `Jna 1:9` And he said to them, "I am a Hebrew, and I fear *_Yahweh_*, the God of heaven, who has made the sea and the dry land."
* `Mic 4:13` "Arise and thresh, O daughter of Zion, for I will make your horn iron and I will make your hooves brass. And you shall beat many people in pieces. And I will consecrate their gain to *_YAHWEH_* and their substance to the Lord of the whole earth."
